#### [97] St. Michael and All Angels (September 29)

**Dietrich's heading (PDF p. 289):** *An Sanct Michaels tag/In dem 91. Psalm.* [On St. Michael's Day. In the 91st Psalm.]

**Verse (PDF pp. 289–290)**

> ER hat seinen Engeln befolhen vber dir/das sie dich behütten auff allen deinen wegen/das sie dich auff den henden tragen/vnd du deinen fuß nit an einen steyn stossest. [p. 290] Auff Löwen vnd Ottern wirdst du gehen/vnnd tretten auff die jungen Löwen vnd Drachen.

He hath given his angels charge over thee, to keep thee in all thy ways; that they bear thee up in their hands, lest thou dash thy foot against a stone. Thou shalt tread upon the lion and adder: the young lion and the dragon shalt thou trample under feet.

**Summary (PDF pp. 290–291)**

> DAs der Sathan vnnd sein gantzes reich vns menschen feind sey/vnd alle augenblick vnns nachstelle/ob er dem leyb oder der Seelen möge schaden thun/solches erfaren wir teglich an vns selb/vñ andern leuten. Denn das so vil jammers vnuersehenlich sich findet/mit kranckheyt/mit vngewitter/mit hagel/mit wasser/mit fewer/vnd anderm/vñ die menschen vntereinander sich so betrüben/beschedigen/vnd allerley vnglück stifften/Solches alles geschicht auß des leydigen Sathans anstifften vñ treyben/Es ist aber darum̃ alles dest beschwerlicher/das der feind vns zu starck ist/vnd wir jm nicht/wie den leuten/können widerstand thun/er fragt weder nach büchssen noch schwert/wir sindt gegen jm bloß/er aber ist vnsichtbar/ [p. 291] vñ wir können jm keinen schaden mit vnser sterck oder macht zufügen. Auff das aber wir vns nit zubeklagen hetten/als hette Gott vnser vergessen/Sind die lieben Engel von Got dazu geordnet/das sie vns bewachen vñ bewaren/vnnd dem feynd widerstandt thun/vnd wehren sollen. Wie Dauid hie sagt/das sie den befelh haben vns zubehüten auff all vnsern wegen/das ist/allenthalb da wir in vnserm beruff gehn/vnd auff den henden zutragen/dz ja kein leid vns widerfare. Solchs sollen alle Christen wissen/vnd nit allein solches beystands vñ hilff sich trösten/vñ teglich drumb bitten/sonder auch all jr leben darnach füren vñ anrichten/das sie ja dises schutzes nit beraubet werdē. Deñ im 34. Psal. stehet das des Herrn Engel sich vmb die her legere/die Got förchtē/Darum̃ wo rohe vngotförchtige menschē sind/die in sündē ligen/vñ nach Gotes wort nicht fragen/bey den selben wöllē die lieben Engel nit sein. Darum̃ sey yederman from̃ vñ förchte Got/so wil Got behüten vñ segnē.

That Satan and his whole kingdom are the enemy of us men, and lie in wait for us every moment to see whether he may do harm to body or soul, we experience daily in ourselves and in other people. For that so much misery comes upon us unforeseen, with sickness, storms, hail, water, fire and other things, and that men so grieve and injure one another and cause all manner of misfortune: all this happens at the instigation and driving of the wretched Satan. And it is all the more grievous because the enemy is too strong for us, and we cannot resist him as we can resist people. He cares nothing for guns or sword; we are defenseless against him, but he is invisible, and we can do him no harm with our strength or might. But so that we should have no cause to complain, as though God had forgotten us, the dear angels are appointed by God to watch over us and keep us, and to resist and restrain the enemy. As David says here, they have the charge to keep us in all our ways, that is, everywhere we go in our calling, and to bear us up in their hands, so that no harm may befall us. All Christians are to know this, and not only comfort themselves with such aid and help and pray for it daily, but also lead and order their whole life accordingly, so that they are not robbed of this protection. For in the 34th Psalm it stands that the angel of the Lord encampeth round about them that fear God. Therefore, where there are coarse, ungodly people, who lie in sins and care nothing for God's word, with them the dear angels will not be. Therefore let everyone be godly and fear God; then God will protect and bless.

**Notes on the verse and summary**
- **The verse** is Psalm 91:11–13 in King James wording. With Luther, Dietrich has the perfect, "He hath given his angels charge" (King James "For he shall give"). The heading's reference, "In dem 91. Psalm," is correct. As at Palm Sunday and Pentecost, the text is not the Gospel of the day (Matthew 18:1–10) but a passage of the Old Testament.
- **The woodcut** (p. 289) shows Revelation 12: the woman clothed with the sun and crowned with stars, the dragon casting out water after her, and angels fighting with swords.
- **"Guns."** *büchssen*, firearms.
- **"Everywhere we go in our calling."** Dietrich's gloss on "in all thy ways," as at Trinity 5 ([51]) and elsewhere: the angels guard us in our station and work.
- **Psalm 34.** Psalm 34:7 (Luther 34:8), in King James wording with Dietrich's "God" for "him." *sich vmb die her legere* is Luther's *lagert sich vmb die her*, "encampeth round about."
- **The prayer** that follows has no "Gebet" rubric (see the note on the German).

**Prayer (PDF p. 292).** There is no "Gebet" rubric: the prayer follows directly after the catchword "Herr" on p. 291.

> HERR Gott himlischer Vatter/Wir danckē dir für deine Vätterliche barmhertzigkeyt/das du deine liebe Engel dazu verordnet hast/das sie auff vns sehen/vnnd wider des leydigen Sathans fürnemen vns schützen sollen/Vnd bitten dich/regier vnnd füre vnsere hertzen vnnd sinn/durch deinen heiligen Geyst dermassen/das wir in deiner forcht vns halten/vnnd wider deinen willen nicht thun/vnnd also in allerley zufallenden nöten/die zuuersicht mögen behalten/das deine liebe Engel werden vmb vns/vnd alles was wir haben/einen starcken vesten zaun machen/das weder der Sathan/noch die arge welt einbrechen/vnnd schaden möge thun/auff das gleich wie durch deinen lieben Sun Christum Jhesum/vns vnsere sünde vergeben/vnnd das ewige leben versprochē ist/also durch den schutz deiner lieben Engel/leyb/leben/vñ alles was wir auff erden haben/möge erhalten vnd befridet werden. Amen.

**New translation**

> Lord God, heavenly Father, we thank Thee for Thy fatherly mercy, that Thou hast ordained Thy dear angels to watch over us and to defend us against the designs of the hateful Satan; and we beseech Thee, so rule and direct our hearts and minds by Thy Holy Ghost, that we may keep ourselves in Thy fear and do nothing against Thy will, and so in all manner of need that befalleth us may retain the confidence that Thy dear angels will make a strong and sure hedge about us and all that we have, so that neither Satan nor the wicked world may break in and do harm; that, even as through Thy dear Son, Jesus Christ, our sins are forgiven us and eternal life is promised us, so through the protection of Thy dear angels body, life, and all that we have on earth may be preserved and protected; *through the same, Thy beloved Son, Jesus Christ our Lord, who liveth and reigneth with Thee and the Holy Ghost, one true God, world without end.* Amen.

**Notes**
- **Rendering choices.**
  - *leydig* means hateful or accursed.
  - *zaun* is a "hedge" (cf. Job 1:10, King James: "made an hedge about him … and about all that he hath").
  - *befridet* means protected; literally "kept in peace," or "fenced about."

#### [98] Sts. Simon and Jude, Apostles (October 28)

**Dietrich's heading (PDF p. 293):** *Am tag Simonis vnd Jude/Johan. am 15.* [On the Day of Simon and Jude. John 15.]

**Verse (PDF pp. 293–294)**

> DAs gebiette ich euch/das jr euch vnter einander liebet/So euch die welt hasset/so wisset das sie mich vor euch gehasset hat/Weret jr [p. 294] von der welt/so hette die welt das jr lieb. Die weil jhr aber nicht von der welt seydt/sonder ich hab euch von der welt erwehlet/Darumb hasset euch die welt.

These things I command you, that ye love one another. If the world hate you, ye know that it hated me before it hated you. If ye were of the world, the world would love his own: but because ye are not of the world, but I have chosen you out of the world, therefore the world hateth you.

**Summary (PDF pp. 294–295)**

> DJß ist ein nötige vermanung vñ trost für die Christen/das erstlich sie vnter einander/alle lieb vnnd freundtligkeyt beweysen/vnd darnach am Creutz sich nicht ergern sollen. Denn vnser vernunfft kan anders nicht vrtheylen/wenn es vns vbel gehet/denn das Gott mit vns zürne/vnnd wölle vnser nicht. Aber da sagts vnsers [sic, for vnser] lieber Herr Christus seinen Jüngern mit hellen worten zuuor/die welt werde sie hassen/Darumb gedencke nur niemand/weñ er recht predigt/das die welt jn hochhalten/loben/ehren/vnd feyren werde/hassen wird sie jn/vnnd wirdt auff den haß/allerley vnglück folgē. Was [p. 295] solt du aber alß denn gedencken? Wilt du es dafür halten/die lehr sey vnrecht/sonst solte die welt sie annemen? Nein. Wilt du aber glauben/Got habe dein vergessen/das er nicht besser vber dir helt/vnd dich so plagen lest? Nein. Sonder das widerspil solt du glauben/vñ das Creutz anders nit annemen noch deuten/Deñ ein gewisses zeychen vnnd zeugniß/das Gott dich von der welt außerwehlet hab/vnnd du nicht ein welt kind/sonder Gottes kind seyest. Derhalb soll niemandt für dem Creutz sich entsetzen/frewen sollen wir vns/als eines gewissen zeugnuß/das vnser lehr recht/vnnd wir Gotes kinder sind/vñ vnsern lohn in yenem leben gewiß empfangen sollen/AMEN.

This is a necessary exhortation and comfort for Christians: first, that they are to show all love and kindness to one another, and then that they are not to be offended at the cross. For when things go ill with us, our reason can judge no otherwise than that God is angry with us and will have nothing to do with us. But here our dear Lord Christ tells His disciples beforehand in plain words that the world will hate them. Therefore let no one think, if he preaches rightly, that the world will hold him in high esteem, praise, honor and celebrate him: it will hate him, and upon the hatred all manner of misfortune will follow. But what are you to think then? Will you hold that the doctrine is wrong, since otherwise the world would accept it? No. Or will you believe that God has forgotten you, since He does not keep better watch over you, and lets you be so plagued? No. But you are to believe the opposite, and to take and interpret the cross in no other way than as a sure sign and testimony that God has chosen you out of the world, and that you are not a child of the world but a child of God. Therefore let no one be dismayed at the cross; rather we are to rejoice in it, as a sure testimony that our doctrine is right, that we are God's children, and that we shall surely receive our reward in the life beyond. Amen.

**Notes on the verse and summary**
- **The verse** is John 15:17–19 in King James wording. Dietrich's "This I command you" (*Das gebiette ich euch*) is the King James "These things I command you."
- **"A child of the world."** *ein welt kind*; compare Luke 16:8, "the children of this world."
- **"Keep better watch over you."** *besser vber dir helt*.
- **"Amen."** The summary ends with "AMEN," as at Epiphany ([12]) and several Trinity Sundays.
- **Misprint.** *vnsers* for *vnser*.
- **The rubric** closes the sanctorale, as "End of the Sunday Gospels" closes the temporale (p. 227, under [71]); here it follows the prayer.

**Prayer (PDF p. 296)**

> HERR Gott hymlischer Vater/der du dich durch deinen Sun Christum Jesum hast offenbaret/Wir bitten dich/gibe deinen heyligen Geyst inn sere [sic, for vnsere] hertzen/das er die rechte Christliche liebe inn vns anzünde/vnnd vor allem dem behütte vnnd beware/das der liebe entgegen ist/Vnnd daneben vns auch tröste vnnd stercke inn aller widerwertigkeyt/so deines worts halb vns begegnet/das wir vns an dein gnad vnd wort vest halten/vnd der welt vnd des Teuffels zorn verachten mögen/Amen.

**New translation**

> Lord God, heavenly Father, who hast revealed Thyself through Thy Son, Jesus Christ: We beseech Thee, give Thy Holy Ghost into our hearts, that He may kindle in us true Christian love, and guard and keep us from all that is contrary to love; and that He may moreover comfort and strengthen us in all adversity which befalleth us for the sake of Thy word, that we may hold fast to Thy grace and word, and despise the wrath of the world and of the devil; *through the same, Thy beloved Son, Jesus Christ our Lord, who liveth and reigneth with Thee and the Holy Ghost, one true God, world without end.* Amen.

**Notes**
- **Supplied object.** *behütte vnnd beware* has no object in the German; "us" is supplied.

**Rubric after the prayer (PDF p. 296)**

> Ende der Euangelien von fürnembsten Festen.

End of the Gospels of the principal feasts.
